# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-10-19 through 2026-10-25  
**Updated:** 2026-10-19T10:45:30+00:00  
**Book-ahead watch:** through 2027-02-16

## Best date plans

### 2026-10-24 — Fall Harvest Festival + Rucola

**Fall Harvest Festival, then a meal.** Northern Italian at Rucola. It is 1.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=990+Washington+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&destination=190+Dean+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.bbg.org/visit/calendar) · [Restaurant](https://rucolabrooklyn.com/)

### 2026-10-23 — Oxomoco + Disco Bambino Presents: An Italian Disco Revolution

**Dinner, then Disco Bambino Presents: An Italian Disco Revolution.** Mexican wood-fired at Oxomoco. It is a 17-minute walk from the venue. Disco Bambino Presents: An Italian Disco Revolution starts at 8:30 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=128+Greenpoint+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11222&destination=80+N+6th+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11249&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.nationalsawdust.org/event/disco-bambino-presents-an-italian-disco-revolution) · [Event booking](https://ci.ovationtix.com/36490/production/1292842) · [Restaurant](https://www.oxomoconyc.com/)

### 2026-10-21 — Rucola + Invisible Architecture: An Evening of Comedy with Charlie Flynn

**Dinner, then Invisible Architecture: An Evening of Comedy with Charlie Flynn.** Northern Italian at Rucola. It is a 14-minute walk from the venue. Doors 7 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=190+Dean+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&destination=702+Union+Street%2C+Brooklyn&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://unionhallny.com/calendar) · [Event booking](https://consumer.pxf.io/APvP5J?u=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.eventbrite.com%2Fe%2Finvisible-architecture-an-evening-of-comedy-with-charlie-flynn-tickets-2002602784838%3Faff%3Ddonyc) · [Restaurant](https://rucolabrooklyn.com/)

### 2026-10-21 — Oxomoco + Anthony Roth Costanzo: How You Get High

**Dinner, then Anthony Roth Costanzo: How You Get High.** Mexican wood-fired at Oxomoco. It is a 17-minute walk from the venue. Anthony Roth Costanzo: How You Get High starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=128+Greenpoint+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11222&destination=80+N.+6th+Street&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.nationalsawdust.org/event/anthony-roth-costanzo-how-you-get-high) · [Event booking](https://ci.ovationtix.com/36490/production/1292545?performanceId=11874034) · [Restaurant](https://www.oxomoconyc.com/)

### 2026-10-25 — Eyval + I Blame Television: A Pop Culture Reading Series

**Dinner, then I Blame Television: A Pop Culture Reading Series.** Persian at Eyval. It is 2.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. I Blame Television: A Pop Culture Reading Series starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=25+Bogart+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11206&destination=900+Fulton+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.sistersbklyn.com/i-blame-televstion-a-pop-culture-reading-series/) · [Restaurant](https://www.eyvalnyc.com/)

### 2026-10-20 — Eyval + Contemporaneous, Recap, and Mantra present: Seeing in the Dark

**Dinner, then Contemporaneous, Recap, and Mantra present: Seeing in the Dark.** Persian at Eyval. It is 2.8 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Contemporaneous, Recap, and Mantra present: Seeing in the Dark starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=25+Bogart+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11206&destination=509+Atlantic+Ave&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://roulette.org/event/contemporaneous-mantra-percussion-recap-percussion-seeing-in-the-dark/) · [Event booking](https://donyc.com/events/2026/10/20/contemporaneous-recap-and-mantra-present-seeing-in-the-dark-tickets) · [Restaurant](https://www.eyvalnyc.com/)

## Where to eat

Verified against captured public evidence and limited follow-up official pages as of 2026-10-18; kept sourced current restaurants, specific timed offers, tasting menus, and dated events, while rejecting duplicate cards and unsupported ordinary-menu duplicates.

Dining research: 2026-10-18T10:32:35+00:00

No Michelin-star claims were kept. Kirbee’s source mentions Michelin-related recognition for Texas pitmaster Chuck Charnichart/Barbs B Q, not a Michelin star for Kirbee’s. Access limits: Motek happy-hour PDFs were not text-readable for item-level prices, Mermaid menu pages exposed only shells, and the NYC community-board PDF was blocked.

### All-you-can-eat Southern buffet with senior and kids pricing

Deals & happy hours — Good Time Country Buffet

From the C as in Charlie/Kisa team, this is a useful affordable sit-down dinner option with fried chicken, collards, mac and cheese, rotating specials, salad bar, and unlimited buffet trips.

Price: $26.99 adults; $23.99 ages 65+; $13.99 kids ages 4-9; desserts, boozy peach frozen drinks, and other extras separate  
Schedule: Wednesday-Sunday, 5:00-10:00 PM  
Conditions: Walk-ins and a limited number of Resy reservations reported by Eater; desserts and boozy peach frozen drinks available separately; tax/tip/service charge, takeout status, and any soda/sweet-tea add-on were not verified in checked sources.  
From: 2026-09-12  
Opened: 2026-09-12  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/413774/good-time-country-buffet-east-village-opening-kisa)

### Five-course Black diaspora tasting menu

Special menus — Creola

Chef Lana Lagomarsini’s 14-seat supper-club-style restaurant is useful for a splurge menu centered on Black culinary history, with dishes such as potlikker consomme and oxtail pate en croute.

Price: $125 tasting menu; optional $65 wine pairing  
Schedule: Wednesday-Saturday, 5:00-11:00 PM; official site says two seatings nightly  
Conditions: Reservations needed for a 14-seat dining room; $125 five-course tasting menu and optional $65 wine pairing per Eater; tax/tip/service charge not stated in checked text; menu rotates seasonally.  
Opened: 2026-09-09  
[Source](https://www.creolanyc.com/)

### Everyday Midtown oyster-bar happy hour window

Deals & happy hours — Mermaid Oyster Bar Times Square

A centrally located official happy-hour window useful before theater or Midtown plans; the checked official page confirms the daily timing but not discounted item prices.

Schedule: Every day, 3:30-6:30 PM  
Conditions: Official location page confirms daily 3:30-6:30 PM happy hour at 127 W. 43rd St.; linked menu page exposed no readable discount/item prices, so do not state oyster, drink, tax/tip/service, or holiday terms.  
[Source](https://www.themermaidnyc.com/mermaid-oyster-bar-times-square/)

### Brazilian Lower East Side opening shaped by Amazonian ingredients

More restaurants — iara

Chef Vitor Mendes’s Brazilian open-kitchen restaurant on the Lower East Side opened October 2, with an official menu listing pão de queijo, tacacá, ceviche com tucupi, moqueca, picanha, frango Amazonas, cupuaçu ice cream, caipirinhas, and Brazilian/Latin American wines, but no posted item prices in captured text.

2026-10-29 · On view October 29–December 12, 2026; opening reception October 29, 6–8pm · art · Free · Paula Cooper Gallery · Chelsea · Manhattan · Score 67.0  
A Paula Cooper group exhibition organized by Ivy Shapiro gathers work by Jennifer Bartlett, David Hammons, Robert Gober, Kazuko Miyamoto, Ree Morton, Elizabeth Murray, and Joel Shapiro around home and autobiography.
